//! NEW inspectable embedding only; semantic evaluation never resolves policy here.
use std::{fs, io, path::Path};
pub const FILES: &[(&str, &[u8])] = &[
    (
        "configs/minimal.json",
        include_bytes!("../data/configs/minimal.json"),
    ),
    (
        "configs/standard.json",
        include_bytes!("../data/configs/standard.json"),
    ),
    (
        "configs/high-rigor.json",
        include_bytes!("../data/configs/high-rigor.json"),
    ),
    (
        "templates/intent.md",
        include_bytes!("../data/templates/intent.md"),
    ),
    (
        "templates/design.md",
        include_bytes!("../data/templates/design.md"),
    ),
    (
        "templates/task-packet.md",
        include_bytes!("../data/templates/task-packet.md"),
    ),
    (
        "templates/implementation-report.md",
        include_bytes!("../data/templates/implementation-report.md"),
    ),
    (
        "templates/validation-report.md",
        include_bytes!("../data/templates/validation-report.md"),
    ),
    (
        "reviewer-protocol.md",
        include_bytes!("../data/reviewer-protocol.md"),
    ),
];
pub fn dump(root: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    for (name, bytes) in FILES {
        let path = root.join(name);
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        fs::write(path, bytes)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}


=== COVERED FILE: fictional-repo/provider/src/evidence.rs; sha256:9d5c4f5e08403986b910de2e6a12fa3e4f61db94dfed7e7243c3aafbb74be6e9 ===
//! NEW fictional evidence reducer: no IO, execution, identity authentication or semantic judging.
use crate::config::Axis;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

fn actor(v: &Value) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    let name = v["name"].as_str()?.to_owned();
    let kind = v["kind"].as_str()?.to_owned();
    if name.trim().is_empty() || !["human", "agent", "script"].contains(&kind.as_str()) {
        return None;
    }
    Some((name, kind))
}
pub fn evaluate(
    records: &[Value],
    gate: &str,
    subject: &str,
    revision: &str,
    version: &str,
    subject_author: &Value,
    axes: &[Axis],
) -> (bool, Vec<Value>) {
    let owner = actor(subject_author);
    let mut all_satisfied = true;
    let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    for axis in axes {
        let mut latest: BTreeMap<(String, String), String> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut malformed = false;
        for record in records {
            if record["kind"] != "review-evidence" {
                continue;
            }
            let data = &record["data"];
            if data["gate"] != gate || data["policy_id"] != axis.id || data["subject"] != subject {
                continue;
            }
            // Missing/malformed revision or config cannot declare stale evidence.
            // Keep author/result/findings conformance AFTER the original stale
            // filters: this repair does not redefine historical eligibility.
            if !data["subject_revision"]
                .as_str()
                .is_some_and(|value| !value.trim().is_empty())
                || !data["config_version"]
                    .as_str()
                    .is_some_and(|value| !value.trim().is_empty())
            {
                malformed = true;
                diagnostics.push(json!({"axis":axis.id,"rule":"malformed-current-evidence","record":record["id"]}));
                continue;
            }
            if data["subject_revision"] != revision {
                diagnostics.push(json!({"axis":axis.id,"rule":"stale-revision","expected":revision,"actual":data["subject_revision"]}));
                continue;
            }
            if data["config_version"] != version {
                diagnostics.push(json!({"axis":axis.id,"rule":"stale-config","expected":version,"actual":data["config_version"]}));
                continue;
            }
            let identity = actor(&data["author"]);
            let conforming = identity.is_some()
                && match data["result"].as_str() {
                    Some("pass") => data["findings"].as_str() == Some(""),
                    Some("fail") => data["findings"]
                        .as_str()
                        .is_some_and(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()),
                    _ => false,
                };
            if !conforming {
                malformed = true;
                diagnostics.push(json!({"axis":axis.id,"rule":"malformed-current-evidence","record":record["id"]}));
                continue;
            }
            let identity = identity.unwrap();
            if owner.as_ref() == Some(&identity) {
                diagnostics.push(json!({"axis":axis.id,"rule":"subject-author-excluded","author":data["author"]}));
                continue;
            }
            // Engine context is durable order. Later conforming same-axis evidence
            // replaces a malformed declaration; exact same-author latest verdict stands.
            malformed = false;
            latest.insert(identity, data["result"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned());
        }
        let fails = latest
            .values()
            .filter(|result| result.as_str() == "fail")
            .count();
        if malformed || fails > 0 || latest.len() < axis.required_authors {
            all_satisfied = false;
            diagnostics.push(json!({"axis":axis.id,"rule":"axis-incomplete-or-failing",
                "required_authors":axis.required_authors,"distinct_authors":latest.len(),
                "standing_fails":fails,"malformed":malformed}));
        }
    }
    (all_satisfied, diagnostics)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn axes() -> Vec<Axis> {
        vec![Axis {
            id: "axis".into(),
            required_authors: 2,
        }]
    }
    fn row(who: &str, kind: &str, result: &str) -> Value {
        json!({"kind":"review-evidence","id":who,"data":{
            "gate":"intent-review","policy_id":"axis","subject":"intent.json",
            "subject_revision":"current","config_version":"fixture-7",
            "author":{"name":who,"kind":kind},"result":result,
            "findings":if result=="pass" {""} else {"Concrete scripted failure"}}})
    }
    fn judge(rows: &[Value]) -> (bool, Vec<Value>) {
        evaluate(
            rows,
            "intent-review",
            "intent.json",
            "current",
            "fixture-7",
            &json!({"name":"owner","kind":"human"}),
            &axes(),
        )
    }
    #[test]
    fn stale_revision_does_not_satisfy_and_names_both_revisions() {
        let mut stale = row("one", "agent", "pass");
        stale["data"]["subject_revision"] = json!("old");
        let (pass, details) = judge(&[stale, row("two", "agent", "pass")]);
        assert!(!pass);
        assert!(details.iter().any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-revision"
            && d["expected"] == "current"
            && d["actual"] == "old"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn stale_config_does_not_satisfy_and_names_both_versions() {
        let mut stale = row("one", "agent", "pass");
        stale["data"]["config_version"] = json!("old");
        let (pass, details) = judge(&[stale, row("two", "agent", "pass")]);
        assert!(!pass);
        assert!(details.iter().any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-config"
            && d["expected"] == "fixture-7"
            && d["actual"] == "old"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn subject_author_identity_requires_exact_name_and_kind_pair() {
        let (pass, details) = judge(&[row("owner", "human", "pass"), row("two", "agent", "pass")]);
        assert!(!pass);
        assert!(details
            .iter()
            .any(|d| d["rule"] == "subject-author-excluded"));
        // Same name, different declared kind is a different unauthenticated identity.
        assert!(judge(&[row("owner", "agent", "pass"), row("two", "agent", "pass")]).0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn n_two_requires_distinct_non_subject_authors_and_rejects_standing_fail() {
        assert!(!judge(&[row("one", "agent", "pass"), row("one", "agent", "pass")]).0);
        assert!(
            !judge(&[
                row("one", "agent", "fail"),
                row("two", "agent", "pass"),
                row("three", "agent", "pass")
            ])
            .0
        );
        assert!(judge(&[row("one", "agent", "pass"), row("two", "agent", "pass")]).0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn latest_same_author_verdict_supersession() {
        assert!(
            judge(&[
                row("one", "agent", "fail"),
                row("one", "agent", "pass"),
                row("two", "agent", "pass")
            ])
            .0
        );
        assert!(
            !judge(&[
                row("one", "agent", "pass"),
                row("one", "agent", "fail"),
                row("two", "agent", "pass")
            ])
            .0
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn malformed_axis_blocks_until_later_conforming_record() {
        let mut bad = row("bad", "agent", "pass");
        bad["data"]["result"] = json!("not-a-verdict");
        let prefix = vec![
            row("one", "agent", "pass"),
            row("two", "agent", "pass"),
            bad,
        ];
        assert!(!judge(&prefix).0);
        let mut repaired = prefix;
        repaired.push(row("three", "agent", "pass"));
        assert!(judge(&repaired).0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_config_after_sufficient_coverage_blocks_until_conforming_repair() {
        let mut missing = row("missing", "agent", "pass");
        missing["data"]
            .as_object_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .remove("config_version");
        let mut records = vec![
            row("one", "agent", "pass"),
            row("two", "agent", "pass"),
            missing,
        ];
        let original = records[2].clone();
        let (allowed, details) = judge(&records);
        assert!(!allowed);
        assert!(details
            .iter()
            .any(|d| d["rule"] == "malformed-current-evidence"));
        assert!(!details.iter().any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-config"));
        records.push(row("missing", "agent", "pass"));
        assert!(judge(&records).0);
        assert_eq!(records[2], original);
    }
    #[test]
    fn missing_or_malformed_required_identity_cannot_masquerade_as_stale() {
        for field in ["config_version", "subject_revision"] {
            for value in [None, Some(Value::Null), Some(json!(7)), Some(json!(""))] {
                let mut bad = row("bad", "agent", "pass");
                match value {
                    Some(value) => bad["data"][field] = value,
                    None => {
                        bad["data"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove(field);
                    }
                }
                let (allowed, details) = judge(&[
                    row("one", "agent", "pass"),
                    row("two", "agent", "pass"),
                    bad,
                ]);
                assert!(!allowed, "{field}: {details:?}");
                assert!(details
                    .iter()
                    .any(|d| d["rule"] == "malformed-current-evidence"));
                assert!(!details
                    .iter()
                    .any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-config" || d["rule"] == "stale-revision"));
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn conforming_wrong_string_version_keeps_coverage_and_never_counts() {
        let mut stale = row("stale", "agent", "pass");
        stale["data"]["config_version"] = json!("old");
        let (allowed, details) = judge(&[
            row("one", "agent", "pass"),
            row("two", "agent", "pass"),
            stale.clone(),
        ]);
        assert!(allowed);
        assert!(details.iter().any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-config"
            && d["expected"] == "fixture-7"
            && d["actual"] == "old"));
        assert!(!judge(&[row("one", "agent", "pass"), stale]).0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn stale_string_keeps_original_author_verdict_filter_order() {
        let mut stale = row("stale", "agent", "pass");
        stale["data"]["config_version"] = json!("old");
        stale["data"]["author"] = Value::Null;
        stale["data"]["result"] = json!("not-a-verdict");
        let (allowed, details) = judge(&[
            row("one", "agent", "pass"),
            row("two", "agent", "pass"),
            stale.clone(),
        ]);
        assert!(allowed);
        assert!(details.iter().any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-config"));
        assert!(!details
            .iter()
            .any(|d| d["rule"] == "malformed-current-evidence"));
        assert!(!judge(&[row("one", "agent", "pass"), stale]).0);
    }
}


=== COVERED FILE: fictional-repo/provider/src/gates.rs; sha256:debd9f188b10779d552aa1271dd8b49447a40d731cb033e3b9a5fa0b8060c122 ===
//! NEW fictional checked routing: deterministic schema denial before evidence.
use crate::{artifacts, config, evidence, protocol, workflow};
use serde_json::Value;

pub fn evaluate(request: &protocol::EvaluateRequest) -> Result<Value, String> {
    if serde_json::to_value(&request.workflow).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? != workflow::topology() {
        return Err("frozen workflow differs from reference topology".into());
    }
    let _ = &request.prior_evaluations; // Transport context is not fabricated source authority.
    let edge = &request.transition;
    if !workflow::contains_transition(&edge.source, &edge.event, &edge.target, &edge.kind)
        || edge.kind != "checked"
    {
        return Ok(protocol::unsupported());
    }
    let profile = config::validate(&request.initial_input)?;
    let name = workflow::subject(&edge.source).ok_or("checked source has no artifact")?;
    let (document, violations) = artifacts::structural(&profile, name)?;
    if !violations.is_empty() {
        return Ok(protocol::deny(
            "software-change-schema-deny",
            "structural violations",
            Value::Array(violations),
        ));
    }
    let document = document.ok_or("structural check produced no document")?;
    if let Some(axes) = profile.policies.get(&edge.source) {
        let revision = document["revision"]
            .as_str()
            .ok_or("subject revision is absent")?;
        let (satisfied, details) = evidence::evaluate(
            &request.context,
            &edge.source,
            name,
            revision,
            &profile.version,
            &document["author"],
            axes,
        );
        if !satisfied {
            return Ok(protocol::deny(
                "software-change-evidence-incomplete",
                "external obligations not satisfied",
                Value::Array(details),
            ));
        }
    }
    // Reference terminal edge is validation-adversarial-review -> end.
    // Its configured validation policy passes the SAME reducer above: no special
    // terminal success shortcut, auto-judge, event sender or workflow persistence.
    Ok(protocol::allow())
}

//! NEW fictional source; one request/process, no external executor.
mod artifacts;
mod config;
mod embedded_data;
mod evidence;
mod gates;
mod protocol;
mod schema;
mod workflow;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::io::{self, Read, Write};

fn main() {
    std::process::exit(run());
}
fn run() -> i32 {
    let arguments: Vec<_> = std::env::args_os().skip(1).collect();
    if arguments.len() == 2 && arguments[0] == "data-dump" {
        return match embedded_data::dump(std::path::Path::new(&arguments[1])) {
            Ok(()) => 0,
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("{error}");
                1
            }
        };
    }
    if !arguments.is_empty() {
        eprintln!("unsupported usage");
        return 2;
    }
    let mut input = String::new();
    if let Err(error) = io::stdin().read_to_string(&mut input) {
        eprintln!("{error}");
        return 2;
    }
    let value: Value = match serde_json::from_str(&input) {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{error}");
            return 2;
        }
    };
    match value["operation"].as_str() {
        Some("describe") => match serde_json::from_value::<protocol::DescribeRequest>(value) {
            Ok(request) if request.operation == "describe" => {
                let _ = request.initial_input; // Approved opaque transport field; topology is static.
                match io::stdout().write_all(workflow::DESCRIBE_BYTES) {
                    Ok(()) => 0,
                    Err(error) => {
                        eprintln!("{error}");
                        2
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {
                eprintln!("invalid describe request");
                2
            }
        },
        Some("evaluate") => match serde_json::from_value::<protocol::EvaluateRequest>(value) {
            Ok(request) if request.operation == "evaluate" => match gates::evaluate(&request) {
                Ok(reply) => match serde_json::to_writer(io::stdout(), &reply) {
                    Ok(()) => 0,
                    Err(error) => {
                        eprintln!("{error}");
                        2
                    }
                },
                Err(error) => {
                    eprintln!("{error}");
                    1
                }
            },
            _ => {
                eprintln!("invalid evaluate request");
                2
            }
        },
        _ => {
            eprintln!("invalid operation");
            2
        }
    }
}

//! NEW fictional concretization: strict flat provider envelopes.
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::{json, Value};

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct DescribeRequest {
    pub operation: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub initial_input: Option<Value>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize, Serialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Transition {
    pub source: String,
    pub event: String,
    pub target: String,
    pub kind: String,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct EvaluateRequest {
    pub operation: String,
    pub workflow: Workflow,
    pub initial_input: Value,
    pub context: Vec<Value>,
    pub transition: Transition,
    pub prior_evaluations: Vec<Value>,
}

pub fn allow() -> Value {
    json!({"result":"allow"})
}
pub fn unsupported() -> Value {
    json!({"result":"unsupported"})
}
pub fn deny(code: &str, message: &str, details: Value) -> Value {
    json!({"result":"deny","feedback":{"code":code,"message":message,"details":details}})
}

    pub initial_state: String,

//! Generic validator for bounded JSON schemas and JSON instances.

#![allow(dead_code)]

use serde_json::{Map, Value};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use std::fmt;

/// Schema types supported by this bounded language.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub(crate) enum SchemaType {
    Object,
    Array,
    String,
}

impl SchemaType {
    fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Object => "object",
            Self::Array => "array",
            Self::String => "string",
        }
    }

    fn from_value(value: &Value) -> Option<Self> {
        match value.as_str() {
            Some("object") => Some(Self::Object),
            Some("array") => Some(Self::Array),
            Some("string") => Some(Self::String),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

/// Keywords permitted on an object schema, in sorted order.
const OBJECT_KEYWORDS: &[&str] = &["additionalProperties", "properties", "required", "type"];
/// Keywords permitted on an array schema, in sorted order.
const ARRAY_KEYWORDS: &[&str] = &["items", "minItems", "type"];
/// Keywords permitted on a string schema, in sorted order.
const STRING_KEYWORDS: &[&str] = &["enum", "minLength", "type"];

/// Return exact keyword allowlist for one schema type.
///
/// This is intentionally kept as one small, inspectable table.  Production
/// acceptance below derives from these tables, so the pin test protects both
/// the declared subset and the validator from accidental subset drift.
fn allowed_keywords(schema_type: SchemaType) -> &'static [&'static str] {
    match schema_type {
        SchemaType::Object => OBJECT_KEYWORDS,
        SchemaType::Array => ARRAY_KEYWORDS,
        SchemaType::String => STRING_KEYWORDS,
    }
}

const ALL_SCHEMA_TYPES: &[SchemaType] =
    &[SchemaType::Object, SchemaType::Array, SchemaType::String];

fn keyword_owner(keyword: &str) -> Option<SchemaType> {
    let mut owner = None;
    let mut owner_count = 0;

    for schema_type in ALL_SCHEMA_TYPES {
        if allowed_keywords(*schema_type).contains(&keyword) {
            owner = Some(*schema_type);
            owner_count += 1;
        }
    }

    // Keywords shared by all schema types (currently only `type`) have no
    // placement owner.  A keyword present in exactly one table is restricted
    // to that schema type; unknown keywords have no owner either.
    if owner_count == 1 {
        owner
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn known_keyword(keyword: &str) -> bool {
    ALL_SCHEMA_TYPES
        .iter()
        .any(|schema_type| allowed_keywords(*schema_type).contains(&keyword))
}

/// A schema-level violation.  These are malformed-schema diagnostics and are
/// kept separate from instance violations at the type level.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct MetaViolation {
    pub(crate) path: String,
    pub(crate) rule: String,
    pub(crate) message: String,
}

impl MetaViolation {
    fn new(path: impl Into<String>, rule: impl Into<String>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            path: path.into(),
            rule: rule.into(),
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for MetaViolation {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(
            formatter,
            "{} at {}: {}",
            self.rule,
            display_path(&self.path),
            self.message
        )
    }
}

/// Report returned when schema meta-validation fails.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct MetaValidationReport {
    pub(crate) violations: Vec<MetaViolation>,
}

impl MetaValidationReport {
    fn new(mut violations: Vec<MetaViolation>) -> Self {
        sort_meta_violations(&mut violations);
        Self { violations }
    }

    pub(crate) fn is_valid(&self) -> bool {
        self.violations.is_empty()
    }

    pub(crate) fn violations(&self) -> &[MetaViolation] {
        &self.violations
    }

    pub(crate) fn into_violations(self) -> Vec<MetaViolation> {
        self.violations
    }
}

/// An instance-level schema violation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct InstanceViolation {
    pub(crate) path: String,
    pub(crate) rule: String,
    pub(crate) message: String,
}

impl InstanceViolation {
    fn new(path: impl Into<String>, rule: impl Into<String>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            path: path.into(),
            rule: rule.into(),
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for InstanceViolation {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(
            formatter,
            "{} at {}: {}",
            self.rule,
            display_path(&self.path),
            self.message
        )
    }
}

/// Report returned after evaluating an instance against a valid schema.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct InstanceReport {
    pub(crate) violations: Vec<InstanceViolation>,
}

impl InstanceReport {
    fn new(mut violations: Vec<InstanceViolation>) -> Self {
        sort_instance_violations(&mut violations);
        Self { violations }
    }

    pub(crate) fn is_valid(&self) -> bool {
        self.violations.is_empty()
    }

    pub(crate) fn violations(&self) -> &[InstanceViolation] {
        &self.violations
    }

    pub(crate) fn into_violations(self) -> Vec<InstanceViolation> {
        self.violations
    }
}

/// Result of checking one schema and one instance.
///
/// `SchemaInvalid` and `InstanceInvalid` cannot be confused by callers.  A
/// valid schema with a valid instance is represented by `Valid`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum CheckResult {
    Valid,
    SchemaInvalid(MetaValidationReport),
    InstanceInvalid(InstanceReport),
}

/// A compiled, meta-valid schema.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct ValidatedSchema {
    root: SchemaNode,
}

impl ValidatedSchema {
    /// Evaluate instance and collect every applicable violation.
    pub(crate) fn evaluate(&self, instance: &Value) -> InstanceReport {
        let mut violations = Vec::new();
        evaluate_node(&self.root, instance, "", &mut violations);
        InstanceReport::new(violations)
    }
}

/// Validate schema meta-rules and compile it for repeated instance checks.
pub(crate) fn validate_schema(schema: &Value) -> Result<ValidatedSchema, MetaValidationReport> {
    let mut violations = Vec::new();
    let root = compile_node(schema, "", &mut violations);
    if violations.is_empty() {
        // `compile_node` returns a node for an object.  For a non-object root,
        // it returns a harmless placeholder while recording an error.
        Ok(ValidatedSchema { root })
    } else {
        Err(MetaValidationReport::new(violations))
    }
}

/// Check schema meta-rules, then evaluate instance when schema is valid.
pub(crate) fn check(schema: &Value, instance: &Value) -> CheckResult {
    match validate_schema(schema) {
        Ok(schema) => {
            let report = schema.evaluate(instance);
            if report.is_valid() {
                CheckResult::Valid
            } else {
                CheckResult::InstanceInvalid(report)
            }
        }
        Err(report) => CheckResult::SchemaInvalid(report),
    }
}

/// Evaluate an instance against a previously validated schema.
pub(crate) fn evaluate_instance(schema: &ValidatedSchema, instance: &Value) -> InstanceReport {
    schema.evaluate(instance)
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct SchemaNode {
    schema_type: SchemaType,
    properties: BTreeMap<String, SchemaNode>,
    required: Vec<String>,
    additional_properties: bool,
    items: Option<Box<SchemaNode>>,
    min_items: Option<u64>,
    min_length: Option<u64>,
    enum_values: Vec<String>,
}

impl SchemaNode {
    fn empty() -> Self {
        Self {
            schema_type: SchemaType::String,
            properties: BTreeMap::new(),
            required: Vec::new(),
            additional_properties: true,
            items: None,
            min_items: None,
            min_length: None,
            enum_values: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

fn compile_node(value: &Value, path: &str, violations: &mut Vec<MetaViolation>) -> SchemaNode {
    let Some(object) = value.as_object() else {
        violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
            path,
            "schema",
            "schema must be a JSON object",
        ));
        return SchemaNode::empty();
    };

    let schema_type = match object.get("type") {
        None => {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                child_path(path, "type"),
                "type",
                "schema must declare `type`",
            ));
            None
        }
        Some(value) => match SchemaType::from_value(value) {
            Some(schema_type) => Some(schema_type),
            None => {
                violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                    child_path(path, "type"),
                    "type",
                    "type must be one of `object`, `array`, or `string`",
                ));
                None
            }
        },
    };

    for keyword in object.keys() {
        if !known_keyword(keyword) {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                child_path(path, keyword),
                "unknown-keyword",
                format!("unknown schema keyword `{keyword}`"),
            ));
            continue;
        }

        if let (Some(schema_type), Some(owner)) = (schema_type, keyword_owner(keyword)) {
            if schema_type != owner {
                violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                    child_path(path, keyword),
                    "keyword-placement",
                    format!(
                        "keyword `{keyword}` is only legal with type `{}`",
                        owner.as_str()
                    ),
                ));
            }
        }
    }

    let properties = compile_properties(object, path, violations);
    let required = compile_required(object, path, &properties, violations);
    let additional_properties = compile_additional_properties(object, path, violations);
    let items = compile_items(object, path, violations);
    let min_items = compile_nonnegative_integer(object, path, "minItems", violations);
    let min_length = compile_nonnegative_integer(object, path, "minLength", violations);
    let enum_values = compile_enum(object, path, violations);

    SchemaNode {
        schema_type: schema_type.unwrap_or(SchemaType::String),
        properties,
        required,
        additional_properties,
        items,
        min_items,
        min_length,
        enum_values,
    }
}

fn compile_properties(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    path: &str,
    violations: &mut Vec<MetaViolation>,
) -> BTreeMap<String, SchemaNode> {
    let Some(value) = object.get("properties") else {
        return BTreeMap::new();
    };
    let Some(properties) = value.as_object() else {
        violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
            child_path(path, "properties"),
            "properties",
            "`properties` must be an object of subschemas",
        ));
        return BTreeMap::new();
    };

    properties
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, schema)| {
            (
                name.clone(),
                compile_node(
                    schema,
                    &child_path(&child_path(path, "properties"), name),
                    violations,
                ),
            )
        })
        .collect()
}

fn compile_required(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    path: &str,
    properties: &BTreeMap<String, SchemaNode>,
    violations: &mut Vec<MetaViolation>,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let Some(value) = object.get("required") else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let Some(required) = value.as_array() else {
        violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
            child_path(path, "required"),
            "required",
            "`required` must be an array of unique property names",
        ));
        return Vec::new();
    };

    let mut names = Vec::new();
    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
    for (index, entry) in required.iter().enumerate() {
        let entry_path = child_path(&child_path(path, "required"), &index.to_string());
        let Some(name) = entry.as_str() else {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                entry_path,
                "required",
                "each `required` entry must be a string",
            ));
            continue;
        };

        if !seen.insert(name.to_owned()) {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                entry_path.clone(),
                "required",
                format!("duplicate required property `{name}`"),
            ));
        }
        if !properties.contains_key(name) {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                entry_path,
                "required",
                format!("required property `{name}` is absent from `properties`"),
            ));
        }
        names.push(name.to_owned());
    }
    names
}

fn compile_additional_properties(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    path: &str,
    violations: &mut Vec<MetaViolation>,
) -> bool {
    let Some(value) = object.get("additionalProperties") else {
        return true;
    };
    if value != &Value::Bool(false) {
        violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
            child_path(path, "additionalProperties"),
            "additionalProperties",
            "`additionalProperties` must be literal `false`",
        ));
        return true;
    }
    false
}

fn compile_items(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    path: &str,
    violations: &mut Vec<MetaViolation>,
) -> Option<Box<SchemaNode>> {
    let value = object.get("items")?;
    if !value.is_object() {
        violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
            child_path(path, "items"),
            "items",
            "`items` must be one subschema object",
        ));
        return None;
    }
    Some(Box::new(compile_node(
        value,
        &child_path(path, "items"),
        violations,
    )))
}

fn compile_nonnegative_integer(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    path: &str,
    keyword: &str,
    violations: &mut Vec<MetaViolation>,
) -> Option<u64> {
    let value = object.get(keyword)?;
    match value.as_u64() {
        Some(number) => Some(number),
        None => {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                child_path(path, keyword),
                keyword,
                format!("`{keyword}` must be an integer greater than or equal to zero"),
            ));
            None
        }
    }
}

fn compile_enum(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    path: &str,
    violations: &mut Vec<MetaViolation>,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let Some(value) = object.get("enum") else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let Some(values) = value.as_array() else {
        violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
            child_path(path, "enum"),
            "enum",
            "`enum` must be a non-empty array of unique strings",
        ));
        return Vec::new();
    };
    if values.is_empty() {
        violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
            child_path(path, "enum"),
            "enum",
            "`enum` must not be empty",
        ));
        return Vec::new();
    }

    let mut result = Vec::new();
    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
    for (index, value) in values.iter().enumerate() {
        let value_path = child_path(&child_path(path, "enum"), &index.to_string());
        let Some(value) = value.as_str() else {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                value_path,
                "enum",
                "each `enum` entry must be a string",
            ));
            continue;
        };
        if !seen.insert(value.to_owned()) {
            violations.push(MetaViolation::new(
                value_path,
                "enum",
                format!("duplicate enum value `{value}`"),
            ));
        }
        result.push(value.to_owned());
    }
    result
}

fn evaluate_node(
    schema: &SchemaNode,
    instance: &Value,
    path: &str,
    violations: &mut Vec<InstanceViolation>,
) {
    if !matches_type(schema.schema_type, instance) {
        violations.push(InstanceViolation::new(
            path,
            "type",
            format!(
                "expected `{}`, found `{}`",
                schema.schema_type.as_str(),
                instance_type_name(instance)
            ),
        ));
        return;
    }

    match schema.schema_type {
        SchemaType::Object => {
            let object = instance
                .as_object()
                .expect("validated schema type and instance type must agree");

            for required in &schema.required {
                if !object.contains_key(required) {
                    violations.push(InstanceViolation::new(
                        path,
                        "required",
                        format!("required property `{required}` is missing"),
                    ));
                }
            }

            if !schema.additional_properties {
                for name in object.keys() {
                    if !schema.properties.contains_key(name) {
                        violations.push(InstanceViolation::new(
                            child_path(path, name),
                            "additionalProperties",
                            format!("property `{name}` is not allowed"),
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }

            for (name, property_schema) in &schema.properties {
                if let Some(value) = object.get(name) {
                    evaluate_node(property_schema, value, &child_path(path, name), violations);
                }
            }
        }
        SchemaType::Array => {
            let array = instance
                .as_array()
                .expect("validated schema type and instance type must agree");
            if let Some(min_items) = schema.min_items {
                let actual = array.len() as u64;
                if actual < min_items {
                    violations.push(InstanceViolation::new(
                        path,
                        "minItems",
                        format!("expected at least {min_items} items, found {actual}"),
                    ));
                }
            }
            if let Some(items) = &schema.items {
                for (index, value) in array.iter().enumerate() {
                    evaluate_node(
                        items,
                        value,
                        &child_path(path, &index.to_string()),
                        violations,
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        SchemaType::String => {
            let string = instance
                .as_str()
                .expect("validated schema type and instance type must agree");
            if let Some(min_length) = schema.min_length {
                let actual = string.chars().count() as u64;
                if actual < min_length {
                    violations.push(InstanceViolation::new(
                        path,
                        "minLength",
                        format!("expected length at least {min_length}, found {actual}"),
                    ));
                }
            }
            if !schema.enum_values.is_empty()
                && !schema
                    .enum_values
                    .iter()
                    .any(|candidate| candidate == string)
            {
                violations.push(InstanceViolation::new(
                    path,
                    "enum",
                    "string is not one of enum values".to_owned(),
                ));
            }
        }
    }
}

fn matches_type(schema_type: SchemaType, instance: &Value) -> bool {
    match schema_type {
        SchemaType::Object => instance.is_object(),
        SchemaType::Array => instance.is_array(),
        SchemaType::String => instance.is_string(),
    }
}

fn instance_type_name(instance: &Value) -> &'static str {
    match instance {
        Value::Null => "null",
        Value::Bool(_) => "boolean",
        Value::Number(number) if number.is_i64() || number.is_u64() => "integer",
        Value::Number(_) => "number",
        Value::String(_) => "string",
        Value::Array(_) => "array",
        Value::Object(_) => "object",
    }
}

fn sort_meta_violations(violations: &mut [MetaViolation]) {
    violations.sort_by(|left, right| {
        (&left.path, &left.rule, &left.message).cmp(&(&right.path, &right.rule, &right.message))
    });
}

fn sort_instance_violations(violations: &mut [InstanceViolation]) {
    violations.sort_by(|left, right| {
        (&left.path, &left.rule, &left.message).cmp(&(&right.path, &right.rule, &right.message))
    });
}

fn child_path(base: &str, segment: &str) -> String {
    format!("{base}/{}", escape_pointer_segment(segment))
}

fn escape_pointer_segment(segment: &str) -> String {
    segment.replace('~', "~0").replace('/', "~1")
}

fn display_path(path: &str) -> &str {
    if path.is_empty() {
        "<root>"
    } else {
        path
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn valid_schema(value: Value) -> ValidatedSchema {
        validate_schema(&value).expect("schema must be valid")
    }

    fn meta_rules(value: Value) -> Vec<String> {
        validate_schema(&value)
            .expect_err("schema must be invalid")
            .violations
            .into_iter()
            .map(|violation| violation.rule)
            .collect()
    }

    fn instance_violations(schema: Value, instance: Value) -> Vec<InstanceViolation> {
        valid_schema(schema).evaluate(&instance).violations
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_rejects_missing_type_at_root_and_nested_levels() {
        let root_rules = meta_rules(json!({}));
        assert!(root_rules.iter().any(|rule| rule == "type"));

        let nested_rules = meta_rules(json!({
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"child": {"type": "array", "items": {}}}
        }));
        assert!(nested_rules.iter().filter(|rule| *rule == "type").count() >= 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_rejects_unknown_keys_recursively() {
        let report = validate_schema(&json!({
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "child": {"type": "object", "unknown": true}
            },
            "unknown": true
        }))
        .expect_err("unknown keywords must be rejected");

        assert_eq!(
            report
                .violations
                .iter()
                .filter(|violation| violation.rule == "unknown-keyword")
                .map(|violation| violation.path.as_str())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec!["/properties/child/unknown", "/unknown"]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_rejects_invalid_type_values() {
        let report = validate_schema(&json!({"type": "number"}))
            .expect_err("unsupported type must be rejected");
        assert_eq!(report.violations.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(report.violations[0].rule, "type");
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_enforces_keyword_placement_for_each_type() {
        let cases = [
            ("object", "items", json!({"type": "array"})),
            ("object", "minItems", json!(0)),
            ("object", "minLength", json!(0)),
            ("object", "enum", json!(["x"])),
            ("array", "properties", json!({})),
            ("array", "required", json!([])),
            ("array", "additionalProperties", json!(false)),
            ("array", "minLength", json!(0)),
            ("array", "enum", json!(["x"])),
            ("string", "properties", json!({})),
            ("string", "required", json!([])),
            ("string", "additionalProperties", json!(false)),
            ("string", "items", json!({"type": "string"})),
            ("string", "minItems", json!(0)),
        ];

        for (schema_type, keyword, value) in cases {
            let mut schema = json!({"type": schema_type});
            schema[keyword] = value;
            let report = validate_schema(&schema).expect_err("misplaced keyword must be rejected");
            assert!(
                report
                    .violations
                    .iter()
                    .any(|violation| violation.rule == "keyword-placement"),
                "missing placement violation for {schema_type}/{keyword}: {:?}",
                report.violations
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_rejects_properties_that_are_not_an_object_of_subschemas() {
        assert!(meta_rules(json!({
            "type": "object",
            "properties": []
        }))
        .contains(&"properties".to_owned()));

        let report = validate_schema(&json!({
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"child": "not a schema"}
        }))
        .expect_err("property value must be a schema object");
        assert!(report
            .violations
            .iter()
            .any(|violation| violation.path == "/properties/child" && violation.rule == "schema"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_rejects_required_shape_duplicates_and_absent_properties() {
        let report = validate_schema(&json!({
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"present": {"type": "string"}},
            "required": ["present", "present", "missing", 1]
        }))
        .expect_err("invalid required entries must be rejected");

        assert!(report.violations.iter().any(
            |violation| violation.rule == "required" && violation.message.contains("duplicate")
        ));
        assert!(report
            .violations
            .iter()
            .any(|violation| violation.rule == "required" && violation.message.contains("absent")));
        assert!(report
            .violations
            .iter()
            .any(|violation| violation.rule == "required" && violation.message.contains("string")));

        assert!(meta_rules(json!({
            "type": "object",
            "required": "present"
        }))
        .contains(&"required".to_owned()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_requires_literal_false_for_additional_properties() {
        for value in [json!(true), json!({"type": "string"}), json!(null)] {
            assert!(meta_rules(json!({
                "type": "object",
                "additionalProperties": value
            }))
            .contains(&"additionalProperties".to_owned()));
        }
        assert!(validate_schema(&json!({
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false
        }))
        .is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_validates_items_and_nested_items() {
        assert!(meta_rules(json!({
            "type": "array",
            "items": []
        }))
        .contains(&"items".to_owned()));
        assert!(meta_rules(json!({
            "type": "array",
            "items": {"type": "object", "properties": {"nested": {}}}
        }))
        .contains(&"type".to_owned()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_validates_nonnegative_integer_keywords() {
        for (keyword, value) in [
            ("minItems", json!(-1)),
            ("minItems", json!(1.5)),
            ("minItems", json!("1")),
            ("minLength", json!(-1)),
            ("minLength", json!(1.5)),
            ("minLength", json!("1")),
        ] {
            let mut schema = json!({
                "type": if keyword == "minItems" { "array" } else { "string" }
            });
            schema[keyword] = value;
            assert!(
                meta_rules(schema).contains(&keyword.to_owned()),
                "invalid {keyword} must be rejected"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn meta_requires_nonempty_unique_string_enum() {
        assert!(meta_rules(json!({"type": "string", "enum": []})).contains(&"enum".to_owned()));
        let report = validate_schema(&json!({
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["one", "one", 2]
        }))
        .expect_err("enum entries must be unique strings");
        assert!(
            report
                .violations
                .iter()
                .filter(|violation| violation.rule == "enum")
                .count()
                >= 2
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn allowlist_pin_matches_normative_subset() {
        let expected = [
            (
                SchemaType::Object,
                ["additionalProperties", "properties", "required", "type"].as_slice(),
            ),
            (SchemaType::Array, ["items", "minItems", "type"].as_slice()),
            (SchemaType::String, ["enum", "minLength", "type"].as_slice()),
        ];
        for (schema_type, keywords) in expected {
            assert_eq!(allowed_keywords(schema_type), keywords);
        }

        let valid_values = [
            ("type", json!("object")),
            ("properties", json!({})),
            ("required", json!([])),
            ("additionalProperties", json!(false)),
            ("items", json!({"type": "string"})),
            ("minItems", json!(0)),
            ("minLength", json!(0)),
            ("enum", json!(["value"])),
        ];
        for (schema_type, keywords) in expected {
            let mut schema = json!({"type": schema_type.as_str()});
            for keyword in keywords {
                if *keyword == "type" {
                    continue;
                }
                let value = valid_values
                    .iter()
                    .find(|(candidate, _)| candidate == keyword)
                    .expect("every allowed keyword has a valid test value")
                    .1
                    .clone();
                schema[*keyword] = value;
            }
            assert!(
                validate_schema(&schema).is_ok(),
                "all allowed keywords must be accepted for {schema_type:?}"
            );
        }

        for (schema_type, forbidden) in [
            (
                "object",
                ["pattern", "minLength", "items", "oneOf", "$ref"].as_slice(),
            ),
            (
                "array",
                ["pattern", "minLength", "properties", "oneOf", "$ref"].as_slice(),
            ),
            (
                "string",
                [
                    "pattern",
                    "items",
                    "minItems",
                    "properties",
                    "oneOf",
                    "$ref",
                ]
                .as_slice(),
            ),
        ] {
            for keyword in forbidden {
                let mut schema = json!({"type": schema_type});
                schema[*keyword] = json!(null);
                assert!(
                    validate_schema(&schema).is_err(),
                    "unsupported keyword `{keyword}` must be rejected"
                );
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn instance_type_mismatch_does_not_cascade() {
        let violations = instance_violations(
            json!({
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["name"],
                "properties": {
                    "name": {"type": "string", "minLength": 3, "enum": ["abc"]}
                },
                "additionalProperties": false
            }),
            json!("wrong type"),
        );
        assert_eq!(violations.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(violations[0].rule, "type");
        assert_eq!(violations[0].path, "");
    }

    #[test]
    fn instance_collects_object_rules_and_nested_properties() {
        let violations = instance_violations(
            json!({
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["name", "kind"],
                "properties": {
                    "name": {"type": "string", "minLength": 3},
                    "kind": {"type": "string", "enum": ["good"]}
                },
                "additionalProperties": false
            }),
            json!({"name": "x", "extra": true}),
        );
        assert_eq!(
            violations
                .iter()
                .map(|violation| (&violation.path, &violation.rule))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec![
                (&"".to_owned(), &"required".to_owned()),
                (&"/extra".to_owned(), &"additionalProperties".to_owned()),
                (&"/name".to_owned(), &"minLength".to_owned()),
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn instance_collects_array_rules_and_type_mismatches_without_cascade() {
        let violations = instance_violations(
            json!({
                "type": "array",
                "minItems": 3,
                "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 2}
            }),
            json!(["", 1]),
        );
        assert_eq!(violations.len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(violations[0].rule, "minItems");
        assert_eq!(violations[1].path, "/0");
        assert_eq!(violations[1].rule, "minLength");
        assert_eq!(violations[2].path, "/1");
        assert_eq!(violations[2].rule, "type");
    }

    #[test]
    fn instance_checks_string_length_and_exact_enum_equality() {
        let short = instance_violations(json!({"type": "string", "minLength": 2}), json!("é"));
        assert_eq!(short.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(short[0].rule, "minLength");

        let wrong = instance_violations(json!({"type": "string", "enum": ["A"]}), json!("a"));
        assert_eq!(wrong.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(wrong[0].rule, "enum");

        let exact = instance_violations(json!({"type": "string", "enum": ["A"]}), json!("A"));
        assert!(exact.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn instance_paths_escape_json_pointer_segments() {
        let violations = instance_violations(
            json!({
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"a/b~c": {"type": "string", "minLength": 2}}
            }),
            json!({"a/b~c": ""}),
        );
        assert_eq!(violations[0].path, "/a~1b~0c");
    }

    #[test]
    fn instance_violation_order_is_deterministic_and_path_rule_keyed() {
        let schema = json!({
            "type": "object",
            "required": ["z", "a"],
            "properties": {
                "z": {"type": "string", "minLength": 4},
                "a": {"type": "array", "minItems": 2, "items": {"type": "string"}}
            },
            "additionalProperties": false
        });
        let instance = json!({"z": "", "a": [false], "x": 1});
        let first = instance_violations(schema.clone(), instance.clone());
        for _ in 0..32 {
            assert_eq!(first, instance_violations(schema.clone(), instance.clone()));
        }

        assert!(first.windows(2).all(|pair| {
            (&pair[0].path, &pair[0].rule, &pair[0].message)
                <= (&pair[1].path, &pair[1].rule, &pair[1].message)
        }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn check_distinguishes_schema_and_instance_failures() {
        assert!(matches!(
            check(&json!({"type": "number"}), &json!(1)),
            CheckResult::SchemaInvalid(_)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            check(&json!({"type": "string"}), &json!(1)),
            CheckResult::InstanceInvalid(_)
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            check(&json!({"type": "string"}), &json!("ok")),
            CheckResult::Valid
        ));
    }
}

//! NEW wrapper around an authentic retained static topology component.
//! The included bytes are unchanged baseline context, not selected policy.
use serde_json::Value;
pub const DESCRIBE_BYTES: &[u8] = include_bytes!("../tests/snapshots/describe.json");
pub fn topology() -> Value {
    serde_json::from_slice(DESCRIBE_BYTES).expect("retained valid workflow JSON")
}
pub fn contains_transition(source: &str, event: &str, target: &str, kind: &str) -> bool {
    topology()["transitions"]
        .as_array()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .any(|t| {
            t["source"] == source
                && t["event"] == event
                && t["target"] == target
                && t["kind"] == kind
        })
}
pub fn subject(state: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
    match state.split('-').next().unwrap_or("") {
        "explore" | "intent" => Some("intent.json"),
        "design" => Some("design.json"),
        "plan" => Some("plan.json"),
        "implement" | "implementation" => Some("implementation-report.json"),
        "validation" => Some("validation-report.json"),
        _ => None,
    }
}

// NEW fictional test source. Scripted records test mechanics, not semantic review.
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::{
    io::Write,
    process::{Command, Stdio},
};

fn invoke(request: &Value) -> (i32, String) {
    let mut child = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_software-change"))
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    child
        .stdin
        .take()
        .unwrap()
        .write_all(request.to_string().as_bytes())
        .unwrap();
    let result = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
    (
        result.status.code().unwrap(),
        String::from_utf8(result.stdout).unwrap(),
    )
}
fn setup(gate: &str) -> (tempfile::TempDir, Value, Value) {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let workflow: Value =
        serde_json::from_slice(include_bytes!("../snapshots/describe.json")).unwrap();
    let edge = workflow["transitions"]
        .as_array()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .find(|t| t["source"] == gate && t["kind"] == "checked")
        .unwrap()
        .clone();
    let name = if gate.starts_with("validation") {
        "validation-report.json"
    } else {
        "intent.json"
    };
    let actor = json!({"name":"subject-author","kind":"human"});
    let schema = json!({"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["revision","author"],
        "properties":{"revision":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"author":{"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,
            "required":["name","kind"],"properties":{"name":{"type":"string","minLength":1},
                "kind":{"type":"string","enum":["human","agent","script"]}}}}});
    let doc = json!({"revision":"r15","author":actor});
    std::fs::write(root.path().join(name), doc.to_string()).unwrap();
    let profile = json!({"config_version":"fixture-7","artifact_root":root.path().to_str().unwrap(),
        "artifact_schemas":{name:schema},"revision_links":[],
        "review_policies":{gate:[{"id":"axis","description":"Scripted fixture axis","required_authors":2}]},"extra":{}});
    let request = json!({"operation":"evaluate","workflow":workflow,"initial_input":profile,
        "context":[],"transition":edge,"prior_evaluations":[]});
    (root, request, doc)
}
fn record(gate: &str, who: &str, result: &str) -> Value {
    let name = if gate.starts_with("validation") {
        "validation-report.json"
    } else {
        "intent.json"
    };
    json!({"id":who,"kind":"review-evidence","data":{"gate":gate,"policy_id":"axis","subject":name,
        "subject_revision":"r15","config_version":"fixture-7","author":{"name":who,"kind":"human"},
        "result":result,"findings":if result=="pass" {""} else {"Scripted material fixture failure"}}})
}
fn reply(request: &Value) -> Value {
    let (exit, stdout) = invoke(request);
    assert_eq!(exit, 0);
    serde_json::from_str(&stdout).unwrap()
}
pub fn exercise(case: &str) {
    let gate = if case.starts_with("terminal") {
        "validation-adversarial-review"
    } else {
        "intent-review"
    };
    let (root, mut request, _doc) = setup(gate);
    match case {
        "describe" => {
            let (exit, stdout) = invoke(&json!({"operation":"describe"}));
            assert_eq!(exit, 0);
            assert_eq!(
                stdout.as_bytes(),
                include_bytes!("../snapshots/describe.json")
            );
        }
        "unknown-field" => {
            request["unknown"] = json!(true);
            let (exit, stdout) = invoke(&request);
            assert_eq!(exit, 2);
            assert!(stdout.is_empty());
        }
        "unsupported" => {
            request["transition"]["event"] = json!("absent-edge");
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "unsupported");
        }
        "schema" => {
            std::fs::write(
                root.path().join("intent.json"),
                r#"{"revision":7,"author":{},"unexpected":"value"}"#,
            )
            .unwrap();
            request["context"] = json!([record(gate, "one", "pass"), record(gate, "two", "pass")]);
            let denied = reply(&request);
            assert_eq!(denied["result"], "deny");
            let details = denied["feedback"]["details"].as_array().unwrap();
            for (path, rule) in [
                ("/revision", "type"),
                ("/author", "required"),
                ("/unexpected", "additionalProperties"),
            ] {
                assert!(
                    details
                        .iter()
                        .any(|d| d["path"] == path && d["rule"] == rule),
                    "{path} {rule}"
                );
            }
            request["context"] = json!([]);
            assert_eq!(denied, reply(&request)); // Schema precedence independent of evidence.
        }
        "absent" => {
            std::fs::remove_file(root.path().join("intent.json")).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(
                reply(&request)["feedback"]["details"][0]["rule"],
                "artifact-readable-json"
            );
        }
        "revision-link" => {
            let schema = request["initial_input"]["artifact_schemas"]["intent.json"].clone();
            request["initial_input"]["artifact_schemas"]["design.json"] = schema;
            request["initial_input"]["artifact_schemas"]["design.json"]["properties"]
                ["intent_revision"] = json!({"type":"string"});
            request["initial_input"]["revision_links"] =
                json!([{"from":"design.json","field":"intent_revision","to":"intent.json"}]);
            std::fs::write(root.path().join("design.json"),
                json!({"revision":"r15","author":{"name":"subject-author","kind":"human"},"intent_revision":"wrong"}).to_string()).unwrap();
            request["transition"] = request["workflow"]["transitions"]
                .as_array()
                .unwrap()
                .iter()
                .find(|edge| edge["source"] == "design" && edge["kind"] == "checked")
                .unwrap()
                .clone();
            let denied = reply(&request);
            assert_eq!(denied["result"], "deny");
            assert!(denied["feedback"]["details"]
                .as_array()
                .unwrap()
                .iter()
                .any(|d| d["rule"] == "revision-link"
                    && d["subject"] == "design.json"
                    && d["target"] == "intent.json"));
        }
        "escape" => {
            #[cfg(unix)]
            {
                let outside = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
                let target = outside.path().join("outside.json");
                std::fs::write(&target, "{}").unwrap();
                std::fs::remove_file(root.path().join("intent.json")).unwrap();
                std::os::unix::fs::symlink(target, root.path().join("intent.json")).unwrap();
                let (exit, stdout) = invoke(&request);
                assert_eq!(exit, 1);
                assert!(stdout.is_empty());
            }
            #[cfg(not(unix))]
            panic!("This fixture proof requires the owner's supported Unix environment");
        }
        "topology" => {
            let schema = request["initial_input"]["artifact_schemas"]["intent.json"].clone();
            for name in [
                "design.json",
                "plan.json",
                "implementation-report.json",
                "validation-report.json",
            ] {
                request["initial_input"]["artifact_schemas"][name] = schema.clone();
                std::fs::write(
                    root.path().join(name),
                    json!({"revision":"r15","author":{"name":"subject-author","kind":"human"}})
                        .to_string(),
                )
                .unwrap();
            }
            let transitions = request["workflow"]["transitions"]
                .as_array()
                .unwrap()
                .clone();
            for edge in transitions {
                request["transition"] = edge.clone();
                let result = reply(&request);
                if edge["kind"] == "checked" {
                    assert!(
                        matches!(result["result"].as_str(), Some("allow" | "deny")),
                        "{edge} {result}"
                    );
                } else {
                    assert_eq!(result["result"], "unsupported");
                }
            }
        }
        "unparseable" => {
            std::fs::write(root.path().join("intent.json"), "not JSON").unwrap();
            assert_eq!(
                reply(&request)["feedback"]["details"][0]["rule"],
                "artifact-readable-json"
            );
        }
        "malformed-replaced" => {
            let mut bad = record(gate, "bad", "pass");
            bad["data"]["result"] = json!("unsupported");
            request["context"] = json!([record(gate, "one", "pass"), bad]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "deny");
            request["context"]
                .as_array_mut()
                .unwrap()
                .push(record(gate, "two", "pass"));
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "allow");
        }
        "pair" => {
            let mut other = record(gate, "one", "pass");
            other["data"]["author"]["kind"] = json!("agent");
            request["context"] = json!([record(gate, "one", "pass"), other]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "allow");
        }
        "other-gate" => {
            request["context"] = json!([
                record("design-review", "one", "pass"),
                record("design-review", "two", "pass")
            ]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "deny");
        }
        "stale-revision" => {
            let mut stale = record(gate, "one", "pass");
            stale["data"]["subject_revision"] = json!("older");
            request["context"] = json!([stale, record(gate, "two", "pass")]);
            let denied = reply(&request);
            assert_eq!(denied["result"], "deny");
            assert!(denied["feedback"]["details"]
                .as_array()
                .unwrap()
                .iter()
                .any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-revision"
                    && d["expected"] == "r15"
                    && d["actual"] == "older"));
        }
        "stale-config" => {
            let mut stale = record(gate, "one", "pass");
            stale["data"]["config_version"] = json!("older");
            request["context"] = json!([stale, record(gate, "two", "pass")]);
            let denied = reply(&request);
            assert_eq!(denied["result"], "deny");
            assert!(denied["feedback"]["details"]
                .as_array()
                .unwrap()
                .iter()
                .any(|d| d["rule"] == "stale-config"
                    && d["expected"] == "fixture-7"
                    && d["actual"] == "older"));
        }
        "self-author" => {
            request["context"] = json!([
                record(gate, "subject-author", "pass"),
                record(gate, "two", "pass")
            ]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "deny");
        }
        "duplicate-author" => {
            request["context"] = json!([record(gate, "one", "pass"), record(gate, "one", "pass")]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "deny");
        }
        "standing-fail" => {
            request["context"] = json!([
                record(gate, "one", "fail"),
                record(gate, "two", "pass"),
                record(gate, "three", "pass")
            ]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "deny");
        }
        "latest" => {
            request["context"] = json!([
                record(gate, "one", "fail"),
                record(gate, "one", "pass"),
                record(gate, "two", "pass")
            ]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "allow");
        }
        "terminal-deficit" => assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "deny"),
        "terminal-complete" | "allow" => {
            request["context"] = json!([record(gate, "one", "pass"), record(gate, "two", "pass")]);
            assert_eq!(reply(&request)["result"], "allow");
        }
        _ => panic!("unknown fixture exercise"),
    }
}

// NEW public data-dump proof; no semantic/model calls.
use std::{fs, process::Command};
#[test]
fn embedded_manifest_exactly_matches_on_disk_data_tree() {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let output = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_software-change"))
        .arg("data-dump")
        .arg(root.path())
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(output.status.success());
    assert!(output.stdout.is_empty());
    for name in [
        "configs/minimal.json",
        "configs/standard.json",
        "configs/high-rigor.json",
        "templates/intent.md",
        "templates/design.md",
        "templates/task-packet.md",
        "templates/implementation-report.md",
        "templates/validation-report.md",
        "reviewer-protocol.md",
    ] {
        assert_eq!(
            fs::read(root.path().join(name)).unwrap(),
            fs::read(
                std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
                    .join("data")
                    .join(name)
            )
            .unwrap()
        );
    }
}

=== COVERED FILE: fictional-repo/provider/tests/linked_parent_escape.rs; sha256:8ecf70e1def6eace97028ba3c0d7009a6d0140d7a40f098aca382e64bebba4bc ===
// NEW regression: linked-parent containment failure retains exit1/no stdout taxonomy.
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::{
    io::Write,
    process::{Command, Stdio},
};
fn invoke(request: &Value) -> std::process::Output {
    let mut child = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_software-change"))
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    child
        .stdin
        .take()
        .unwrap()
        .write_all(request.to_string().as_bytes())
        .unwrap();
    child.wait_with_output().unwrap()
}
#[test]
fn escaping_linked_parent_is_provider_error_while_missing_mismatch_and_valid_keep_taxonomy() {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let outside = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let actor = json!({"name":"fixture","kind":"script"});
    let base = json!({"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,
        "required":["revision","author"],"properties":{"revision":{"type":"string","minLength":1},
        "author":{"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["name","kind"],
        "properties":{"name":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"kind":{"type":"string","enum":["human","agent","script"]}}}}});
    let mut design_schema = base.clone();
    design_schema["properties"]["intent_revision"] = json!({"type":"string"});
    let workflow: Value =
        serde_json::from_slice(include_bytes!("snapshots/describe.json")).unwrap();
    let edge = workflow["transitions"]
        .as_array()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .find(|e| e["source"] == "design" && e["event"] == "design-ready")
        .unwrap()
        .clone();
    let request = json!({"operation":"evaluate","workflow":workflow,"context":[],
        "initial_input":{"config_version":"fixture-7","artifact_root":root.path().to_str().unwrap(),
        "artifact_schemas":{"intent.json":base,"design.json":design_schema},"review_policies":{},
        "revision_links":[{"from":"design.json","field":"intent_revision","to":"intent.json"}],"extra":{}},
        "transition":edge,"prior_evaluations":[]});
    std::fs::write(
        root.path().join("design.json"),
        json!({"revision":"r1","author":actor,"intent_revision":"r1"}).to_string(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let parent = root.path().join("intent.json");
    let expect_link_denial = |output: std::process::Output| {
        assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(0));
        let value: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&output.stdout).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(value["result"], "deny");
        assert!(value["feedback"]["details"]
            .as_array()
            .unwrap()
            .iter()
            .any(|v| v["rule"] == "revision-link"
                && v["subject"] == "design.json"
                && v["target"] == "intent.json"));
    };
    expect_link_denial(invoke(&request)); // Ordinary absent parent stays schema denial.
    std::fs::write(&parent, json!({"revision":"different"}).to_string()).unwrap();
    expect_link_denial(invoke(&request)); // Ordinary mismatch stays schema denial.
    std::fs::remove_file(&parent).unwrap();
    let external = outside.path().join("intent.json");
    std::fs::write(&external, json!({"revision":"r1"}).to_string()).unwrap();
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&external, &parent).unwrap();
        let output = invoke(&request);
        assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(1));
        assert!(output.stdout.is_empty());
        assert!(String::from_utf8(output.stderr)
            .unwrap()
            .contains("artifact intent.json escapes configured root"));
        std::fs::remove_file(&parent).unwrap();
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    panic!("Owner's supported Unix fixture environment is required");
    std::fs::write(&parent, json!({"revision":"r1"}).to_string()).unwrap();
    let output = invoke(&request);
    assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(0));
    assert_eq!(
        serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&output.stdout).unwrap(),
        json!({"result":"allow"})
    );
}


=== COVERED FILE: fictional-repo/provider/tests/opaque_describe.rs; sha256:6608df346704874b7b5aa63eb4da35443426cf2cb88aed6014245dd8b4ac763a ===
// Approved A: opaque optional initial_input does not alter static snapshot bytes.
use serde_json::json;
use std::{
    io::Write,
    process::{Command, Stdio},
};
fn invoke(value: serde_json::Value) -> std::process::Output {
    let mut child = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_software-change"))
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    child
        .stdin
        .take()
        .unwrap()
        .write_all(value.to_string().as_bytes())
        .unwrap();
    child.wait_with_output().unwrap()
}
#[test]
fn optional_opaque_initial_input_is_ignored_without_allowing_other_fields() {
    for value in [
        json!(null),
        json!(false),
        json!([1]),
        json!({"unknown":"opaque"}),
    ] {
        let result = invoke(json!({"operation":"describe","initial_input":value}));
        assert!(result.status.success());
        assert_eq!(
            result.stdout.as_slice(),
            include_bytes!("snapshots/describe.json")
        );
    }
    let result = invoke(json!({"operation":"describe","initial_input":{},"other":1}));
    assert_eq!(result.status.code(), Some(2));
    assert!(result.stdout.is_empty());
}

=== COVERED FILE: fictional-repo/provider/tests/shipped_data.rs; sha256:579975d08da961d1916bcc093de32b9d3858e4ae598246462b8d2457d09b741f ===
#![allow(dead_code)]
#[path = "../src/config.rs"]
mod config;
#[path = "../src/schema.rs"]
mod schema;
#[path = "../src/workflow.rs"]
mod workflow;
#[test]
fn all_profiles_pass_production_config_validation_and_have_exact_subjects() {
    for text in [
        include_str!("../data/configs/minimal.json"),
        include_str!("../data/configs/standard.json"),
        include_str!("../data/configs/high-rigor.json"),
    ] {
        let input: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        let profile = config::validate(&input).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            profile
                .schemas
                .keys()
                .map(String::as_str)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec![
                "design.json",
                "implementation-report.json",
                "intent.json",
                "plan.json",
                "validation-report.json"
            ]
        );
        for (gate, axes) in profile.policies {
            for axis in axes {
                let original = input["review_policies"][&gate]
                    .as_array()
                    .unwrap()
                    .iter()
                    .find(|a| a["id"] == axis.id)
                    .unwrap();
                assert_eq!(
                    axis.required_authors,
                    original
                        .get("required_authors")
                        .map(|v| v.as_u64().unwrap() as usize)
                        .unwrap_or(1)
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn profiles_carry_exact_shipped_profile_mapping_and_author_counts() {
    let cases = [
        (
            "minimal",
            "minimal-6",
            1usize,
            include_str!("../data/configs/minimal.json"),
        ),
        (
            "standard",
            "standard-7",
            22,
            include_str!("../data/configs/standard.json"),
        ),
        (
            "high-rigor",
            "high-rigor-7",
            46,
            include_str!("../data/configs/high-rigor.json"),
        ),
    ];
    for (_name, version, total, raw) in cases {
        let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(raw).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(value["config_version"], version);
        let configured = config::validate(&value).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(configured.version, version);
        assert_eq!(
            configured.policies.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>(),
            total
        );
        for (gate, axes) in configured.policies {
            assert!(workflow::subject(&gate).is_some());
            for axis in axes {
                let original = value["review_policies"][&gate]
                    .as_array()
                    .unwrap()
                    .iter()
                    .find(|v| v["id"] == axis.id)
                    .unwrap();
                let declared = original
                    .get("required_authors")
                    .map(|v| v.as_u64().unwrap() as usize)
                    .unwrap_or(1);
                assert!(declared > 0);
                assert_eq!(axis.required_authors, declared);
            }
        }
    }
}
#[test]
fn every_axis_prompt_references_subject_template_schema_and_all_antipedantry_guards() {
    for (name, raw) in [
        ("minimal", include_str!("../data/configs/minimal.json")),
        ("standard", include_str!("../data/configs/standard.json")),
        (
            "high-rigor",
            include_str!("../data/configs/high-rigor.json"),
        ),
    ] {
        let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(raw).unwrap();
        for (gate, axes) in value["review_policies"].as_object().unwrap() {
            let subject = workflow::subject(gate).unwrap();
            let template = match subject {
                "plan.json" => "task-packet.md",
                "intent.json" => "intent.md",
                "design.json" => "design.md",
                "implementation-report.json" => "implementation-report.md",
                "validation-report.json" => "validation-report.md",
                _ => panic!("unmapped canonical subject"),
            };
            for axis in axes.as_array().unwrap() {
                let prompt = axis["example_prompt"].as_str().unwrap();
                assert!(
                    prompt.contains(&format!("templates/{template}")),
                    "{gate} {}",
                    axis["id"]
                );
                assert!(
                    prompt.contains(&format!("configs/{name}.json#/artifact_schemas/{subject}"))
                );
                for guard in [
                    "operating_context",
                    "threat_boundary",
                    "outside_obligations",
                    "minor blemishes",
                    "length/count proxies",
                    "silence",
                    "invented norms",
                    "Do not waive material finding",
                ] {
                    assert!(
                        prompt.contains(guard),
                        "{gate}/{} lacks {guard}",
                        axis["id"]
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
