=== COVERED FILE: fictional-repo/scripts/assert-doc-authority.py; sha256:e7fb99420d799329142ed4ba5200ef4eb2c264df2aff71670648a9f12682c997 ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Check fictional repository document authority and relative Markdown links."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ACCEPTANCE_REQUIREMENTS = (
    "A caller can inspect the frozen policies and artifact schemas before evaluating a transition.",
    "A malformed subject artifact is denied with each violated structural rule named.",
    "A checked transition refuses stale, self-authored, duplicate-author, or configured-obligation-incomplete evidence.",
    "The reference workflow reaches its terminal state only after its configured validation obligations are satisfied.",
)
MARKDOWN_LINK = re.compile(r"(?<!!)\[[^\]]+\]\(([^)\s]+)(?:\s+['\"][^)]*['\"])?\)")


def markdown_links_resolve(root: Path) -> None:
    root = root.resolve()
    for document in sorted(root.rglob("*.md")):
        if not document.is_file():
            continue
        for target in MARKDOWN_LINK.findall(document.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if target.startswith(("#", "/", "//", "http:", "https:", "mailto:")):
                continue
            target = target.split("#", 1)[0]
            if not target:
                continue
            resolved = (document.parent / target).resolve()
            try:
                resolved.relative_to(root)
            except ValueError as error:
                raise ValueError(f"link escapes companion root: {document.relative_to(root)} -> {target}") from error
            if not resolved.is_file():
                raise ValueError(f"missing Markdown link target: {document.relative_to(root)} -> {target}")


def check_documents(root: Path) -> None:
    authoritative = root / "docs" / "PRD.md"
    supplemental = root / "loop-engine-software-change-provider-prd.md"
    matrix = root / "implementation-evidence" / "requirement-to-proof.md"
    repository_readme = root / "README.md"
    provider_readme = root / "provider" / "README.md"
    for path in (authoritative, supplemental, matrix, repository_readme, provider_readme):
        if not path.is_file():
            raise ValueError(f"required companion missing: {path.relative_to(root)}")

    authoritative_text = authoritative.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    supplemental_text = supplemental.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if "authoritative" not in authoritative_text.lower():
        raise ValueError("docs/PRD.md does not declare authority")
    if "supplemental" not in supplemental_text.lower() or "non-authoritative" not in supplemental_text.lower():
        raise ValueError("supplemental change PRD does not declare supplemental/non-authoritative status")

    repository_text = repository_readme.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    provider_text = provider_readme.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for text, links in (
        (repository_text, ("docs/PRD.md", "implementation-evidence/requirement-to-proof.md")),
        (provider_text, ("../docs/PRD.md", "../implementation-evidence/requirement-to-proof.md")),
    ):
        for link in links:
            if f"]({link})" not in text:
                raise ValueError(f"README missing required link {link}")

    matrix_text = matrix.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for requirement in ACCEPTANCE_REQUIREMENTS:
        if requirement not in matrix_text:
            raise ValueError(f"proof matrix missing acceptance requirement: {requirement}")


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("root", nargs="?", type=Path, help="fictional repository root")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    root = args.root if args.root is not None else Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    try:
        root = root.resolve()
        if not root.is_dir():
            raise ValueError(f"companion root is not a directory: {root}")
        markdown_links_resolve(root)
        check_documents(root)
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError) as error:
        print(f"assert-doc-authority: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    print("assert-doc-authority: passed")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


=== COVERED FILE: fictional-repo/scripts/assert-requirement-proof.py; sha256:35ebc42990ff99b1ba0a7c8c9e7dc1cb98df2eb6b79d45cd692aca4efcfb37d5 ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""NEW four-acceptance exact map/inventory + hash-bound genuine proof checker."""
import argparse
import ast
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re

REQUIREMENTS = (
    "A caller can inspect the frozen policies and artifact schemas before evaluating a transition.",
    "A malformed subject artifact is denied with each violated structural rule named.",
    "A checked transition refuses stale, self-authored, duplicate-author, or configured-obligation-incomplete evidence.",
    "The reference workflow reaches its terminal state only after its configured validation obligations are satisfied.",
)


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def check(root, receipt_path, binding_path, repair_path, evidence_path):
    inventory = json.loads((root / "implementation-evidence/source-inventory.json").read_text())
    mapping = json.loads((root / "implementation-evidence/four-acceptance-proof-map.json").read_text())
    assert [row["requirement"] for row in mapping] == list(REQUIREMENTS), "four exact acceptance rows"
    matrix = (root / "implementation-evidence/requirement-to-proof.md").read_text()
    references = set()
    for expected in mapping:
        rows = [line.split("|") for line in matrix.splitlines()
                if line.startswith("| " + expected["requirement"] + " |")]
        assert len(rows) == 1, "missing/duplicate exact matrix requirement"
        actual = re.findall(r"`([^`]+)`", rows[0][2])
        assert actual == expected["proofs"], "matrix proof names disagree"
        references.update(actual)
    assert references == set(inventory["proof_inventory"]), "proof inventory differs"
    for reference in references:
        filename, name = reference.split("::", 1)
        path = root / filename
        assert path.is_file(), "proof source absent"
        if path.suffix == ".rs":
            assert re.search(r"fn\s+" + re.escape(name) + r"\s*\(", path.read_text()), "Rust proof name absent"
        else:
            tree = ast.parse(path.read_text())
            assert any(isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name == name
                       for node in ast.walk(tree)), "Python proof name absent"
    actual_files = {str(p.relative_to(root)): sha(p) for p in sorted(root.rglob("*"))
                    if p.is_file() and p.name != "source-inventory.json"}
    assert actual_files == inventory["files_sha256"], "covered source inventory differs"
    journey = (root / "scripts/production-journey.py").read_text()
    prefix = "for record in good[:-1]:"
    repair = "append(missing)"
    assert prefix in journey and repair in journey, "public journey lacks actual deficit/repair"
    assert journey.index(prefix) < journey.index("for defect in") < journey.index(repair), "public journey preloads coverage"

    binding = json.loads(binding_path.read_text())
    assert sha(receipt_path) == binding["runtime_receipt_sha256"], "runtime receipt identity mismatch"
    receipt = json.loads(receipt_path.read_text())
    assert receipt["status"] == "passed" and len(receipt["journeys"]) == 3
    assert all(j["state"] == "end" and j["history"] for j in receipt["journeys"])
    assert receipt["fixture_before"] == receipt["fixture_after"] == binding["v8_source_sha256"]
    assert receipt["provider_sha256"] == binding["provider_sha256"]
    for filename, expected in binding["unchanged_runtime_files"].items():
        assert sha(root / filename) == expected, "runtime input changed"
    for filename, entry in binding["runtime_prefixes"].items():
        if filename in binding["focused_evidence_runtime_changes"]:
            continue  # Original v8 prefix remains historical; actual v13 proof binds current bytes below.
        content = (root / filename).read_bytes()
        assert hashlib.sha256(content[:entry["bytes"]]).hexdigest() == entry["sha256"], "runtime body changed"
        assert content[entry["bytes"]:].decode().lstrip().startswith(entry["test_only_marker"])
    assert (root / "scripts/production-journey.py").read_bytes() == Path(binding["v8_journey"]).read_bytes()
    repair = json.loads(repair_path.read_text())
    assert repair["status"] == "passed" and len(repair["cases"]) == 2
    assert repair["cases"][0]["label"] == "old-red" and repair["cases"][0]["contract"]["contract_pass"] is False
    assert repair["cases"][1]["label"] == "new-green" and repair["cases"][1]["contract"]["contract_pass"] is True
    assert repair["cases"][1]["corrected_continuation"] == "design-review"
    assert repair["current_source_sha256"] == binding["focused_proof_source_sha256"], "original repair execution source differs"
    current_source = {str(p.relative_to(root)): sha(p) for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")) if p.is_file()}
    changed_since_repair = {name for name in set(current_source) | set(repair["current_source_sha256"])
                            if current_source.get(name) != repair["current_source_sha256"].get(name)}
    allowed_docs = {"implementation-evidence/four-acceptance-proof-map.json",
                    "implementation-evidence/requirement-to-proof.md",
                    "implementation-evidence/source-inventory.json", "scripts/assert-requirement-proof.py"}
    evidence = json.loads(evidence_path.read_text())
    assert sha(evidence_path) == binding["evidence_proof_receipt_sha256"], "evidence receipt identity mismatch"
    assert evidence["status"] == "passed" and evidence["contract_pass"] is True
    assert evidence["source_before"] == evidence["source_after"] == binding["evidence_proof_source_sha256"], "original evidence execution source differs"
    expected_cases = [("valid-control", "completed", False),
                      ("missing-config-restoration", "rejected", True),
                      ("stale-with-sufficient", "completed", False),
                      ("stale-noncounting", "rejected", False),
                      ("stale-malformed-order-unchanged", "completed", False),
                      ("standing-fail-and-own-restoration", "rejected", True)]
    assert [(case["case"], case["event_status"], case["same_author_restoration"])
            for case in evidence["cases"]] == expected_cases, "evidence scenario outcomes differ"
    assert all(case["context_unchanged"] is True for case in evidence["cases"])
    # Only this documented successor is additional to the original four paths.
    # Bind its actual bytes at both genuine proof sources and the current source;
    # do not edit either historical receipt or infer its absent source fields.
    current_documents = binding.get("current_document_bindings", {})
    assert isinstance(current_documents, dict), "current document bindings must be an object"
    assert set(current_documents) <= {"loop-engine-software-change-provider-task-packets.md"}, "unsupported current document path"
    for filename, entry in current_documents.items():
        assert entry == {
            "repair_source_sha256": repair["current_source_sha256"][filename],
            "evidence_source_sha256": evidence["source_before"][filename],
            "current_source_sha256": current_source[filename],
        }, "current document binding differs from genuine proof or current source"
        allowed_docs.add(filename)
    changed_since_evidence = {name for name in set(current_source) | set(evidence["source_before"])
                             if current_source.get(name) != evidence["source_before"].get(name)}
    assert changed_since_evidence <= allowed_docs, "runtime/test/data changed after evidence proof"
    assert set(binding["document_only_successor_paths"]) == allowed_docs
    assert set(binding["focused_evidence_runtime_changes"]) == {"provider/src/evidence.rs"}
    assert changed_since_repair <= allowed_docs | {"provider/src/evidence.rs"}, "unproved runtime changes after original repair"
    for filename, entry in binding["focused_evidence_runtime_changes"].items():
        assert repair["current_source_sha256"][filename] == entry["before_sha256"]
        assert sha(root / filename) == evidence["source_before"][filename] == entry["after_sha256"]
        assert entry["scope"] == "required revision/config shape guard only, original stale author/verdict order preserved"
    for filename, entry in binding["focused_runtime_changes"].items():
        assert sha(root / filename) == entry["after_sha256"]
        assert entry["scope"] == "propagate linked-parent containment error; ordinary parent mismatch/missing unchanged"
    print("four exact acceptance rows, actual named source inventory, deficit/repair, and genuine runtime receipt agree; not semantic review")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--receipt", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--binding", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--repair-proof", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--evidence-proof", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    if json.loads(args.binding.read_bytes()).get("generation") == "new-current-01":
        from assert_new_current_proof import validate
        print(json.dumps(validate(root, args.receipt, args.binding, args.repair_proof, args.evidence_proof)))
    else:
        check(root, args.receipt, args.binding, args.repair_proof, args.evidence_proof)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def need(v,m):

def sha(p): return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def inventory(root): return {str(p.relative_to(root)):sha(p) for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")) if p.is_file()}

def validate(root,receipt_path,binding_path,repair_path,evidence_path,scripted=False):
    root=Path(root).resolve(strict=True); receipt_path=Path(receipt_path).resolve(strict=True); out=receipt_path.parent
    b=load(binding_path); r=load(receipt_path); linked=load(repair_path); identity=load(evidence_path)
    mode="scripted" if scripted else "native"
    for obj in [b,r,linked,identity]:
        need(obj["generation"]=="new-current-01" and obj["backend"]==mode,"generation/backend mismatch")
    need(r["status"]==("scripted-complete" if scripted else "passed"),"current proof incomplete")
    need(r["mechanical_only"] is True and r["semantic_review"] is False,"semantic judgment claim")
    need(b["runtime_sha256"]==sha(receipt_path) and b["identity_sha256"]==sha(evidence_path) and b["linked_sha256"]==sha(repair_path),"receipt identity mismatch")
    src=inventory(root)

    need(b["engine_sha256"]==r["engine_sha256"]==ENGINE_SHA,"frozen engine mismatch")
    provider=Path(r["provider_path"]).resolve(strict=True)
    need(provider.is_relative_to(out/"build") and sha(provider)==b["provider_sha256"]==r["provider_sha256"],"provider identity mismatch")
    index=load(root/"implementation-evidence/source-inventory.json")
    need(index["files_sha256"]=={k:v for k,v in src.items() if Path(k).name!="source-inventory.json"},"source inventory mismatch")
    mapping=load(root/"implementation-evidence/four-acceptance-proof-map.json")
    need([v["requirement"] for v in mapping]==[
        "A caller can inspect the frozen policies and artifact schemas before evaluating a transition.",
        "A malformed subject artifact is denied with each violated structural rule named.",
        "A checked transition refuses stale, self-authored, duplicate-author, or configured-obligation-incomplete evidence.",
        "The reference workflow reaches its terminal state only after its configured validation obligations are satisfied."], "four exact rows")
    matrix=(root/"implementation-evidence/requirement-to-proof.md").read_text(); refs=set()

        need(len(lines)==1 and re.findall(chr(96)+"([^"+chr(96)+"]+)"+chr(96),lines[0][2])==row["proofs"],"matrix proof mismatch")
        refs.update(row["proofs"])

        else: need(any(isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name==name for n in ast.walk(ast.parse(text))),"Python proof absent")
    need(r["protocol"]==["describe-exact","opaque-describe-exact","unexpected-envelope-refused","unknown-operation-refused","data-dump-exact"],"protocol controls incomplete")
    need([j["profile"] for j in r["journeys"]]==["minimal","standard","high-rigor"],"profiles incomplete/duplicate")
    for j in r["journeys"]:
        f=j["final_show"]; submitted=load(root/("provider/data/configs/"+j["profile"]+".json"))
        need({k:v for k,v in f["initial_input"].items() if k!="artifact_root"}==submitted and set(f["initial_input"])==set(submitted)|{"artifact_root"},"frozen input mismatch")
        need(Path(f["initial_input"]["artifact_root"]).resolve().is_relative_to(out),"artifact root escaped")
        need(f["current_state"]=="end" and f["lifecycle"]=="final" and f["completion_mode"]=="completed" and
             f["has_overrides"] is False and f["override_count"]==0 and f["requestable_events"]==[],"terminal/override mismatch")
        history=j["history"]; seq=[x["sequence"] for x in history]
        need(history and seq==sorted(set(seq)) and any(x["action"]["kind"]=="transition" and x["action"]["transition"]["target"]=="end"
             and x["action"]["outcome"]["outcome"]=="committed" for x in history),"terminal history incomplete")
        required={"stale-revision","stale-config","subject-author"}

    names=["valid-control","stale-with-sufficient","stale-noncounting","stale-malformed-order-unchanged","standing-fail-and-own-restoration"]

        if expected=="rejected":
            need(c["before"]["context"]==c["after"]["context"] and c["before"]["current_state"]==c["after"]["current_state"],"refusal mutated state/context")
            need(c["restoration"]["status"]=="completed","conforming restoration absent")

        need(row["environment_limits"]=={"CARGO_BUILD_JOBS":"2","RUST_TEST_THREADS":"2","RAYON_NUM_THREADS":"2","OMP_NUM_THREADS":"2"},"thread limits differ")
    def observed(index):
        need(type(index) is int and 1<=index<=len(commands),"invalid observation index")
        return load(out/"commands"/("%04d"%index)/"stdout.txt")
    for j in r["journeys"]:
        need(observed(j["final_show_index"])["result"]==j["final_show"] and observed(j["history_index"])["result"]==j["history"],"terminal summary not actual captured output")

        for field in ["event","restoration"]:

            if v is not None:
                need(observed(v["_command_index"])=={k:x for k,x in v.items() if k!="_command_index"},"identity summary not actual captured output")
    need([c["case"] for c in linked["cases"]]==["missing","mismatch","escape","valid"],"linked case set differs")
    for c in linked["cases"]:
        i=c["command_index"]; row=commands[i-1]; d=out/"commands"/("%04d"%i)
        need(row["argv"]==[str(provider)],"linked proof wrong invocation")
        if c["case"]=="escape":
            need(row["exit_code"]==1 and (d/"stdout.txt").read_bytes()==b"" and "artifact intent.json escapes configured root" in (d/"stderr.txt").read_text(),"containment taxonomy")
        else:

    return {"status":"SCRIPTED_ADAPTER_PATH_ONLY" if scripted else "GENUINE_CURRENT_MECHANICAL_PROOF","native_proof":not scripted,"semantic_review":False,"commands":len(commands)}

"""Root-owned v6 fictional proof: separate build + real engine, never paid calls."""
import argparse

import json

import subprocess
import sys

ENGINE_SHA = "e1599f645f93d0b3a8d64403253f74e0d3e956145b80aa791f57cc3f752723bc"
SNAPSHOT_SHA = "2361843677d68b90775db42939f6333f16760138bfd36c96a71d5f01a65498e2"


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def inventory(root):
    return {str(p.relative_to(root)): sha(p) for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")) if p.is_file()}


def write(path, value):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


def sample(schema):
    kind = schema["type"]
    if kind == "object":
        return {key: sample(schema["properties"][key]) for key in schema.get("required", [])}
    if kind == "array":
        return [sample(schema["items"]) for _ in range(max(1, schema.get("minItems", 0)))]
    return schema.get("enum", ["Scripted fictional proof value"])[0]


def main():

    parser.add_argument("--fixture", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--engine", type=Path, required=True)

                    append(missing)
                    cli("show", rid, "--view", "full")
                cli("event", rid, edge["event"])
                transition_count += 1
                assert transition_count <= 30, "unexpected topology"
        receipt["fixture_after"] = inventory(fixture)
        assert receipt["fixture_after"] == before
        assert sha(engine) == ENGINE_SHA and sha(provider) == receipt["provider_sha256"]
        assert len(receipt["journeys"]) == 3
        assert {str(path): sha(path) for path in reuse_path.parent.glob("run.sqlite*") if path.is_file()} == old_catalog_hashes
        assert {str(path): sha(path) for directory in preserved["failed_proof_directories"]
                for path in Path(directory).glob("run.sqlite*") if path.is_file()} == failed_catalog_hashes
        receipt["preserved_failed_catalogs_unchanged"] = True
        receipt["old_catalog_unchanged"] = True
        receipt["status"] = "passed"
    except BaseException as error:
        receipt.update(status="failed", failure=repr(error))
        raise
    finally:
        write(output / "receipt.json", receipt)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
